Complementary to this feature-level motion analysis, OCD SLAM integrates a 3D object detection module (SMOKE) with Kalman filter-based object tracking to perform object-level motion classification, enabling robust separation of static and dynamic scene elements for accurate pose estimation. The proposed approach has been evaluated on various sequences from the KITTI Odometry and KITTI Raw datasets. Results demonstrate that OCD SLAM achieves significant improvement in trajectory accuracy compared to ORB-SLAM2 and several state-of-the-art dynamic SLAM methods. Ablation studies further demonstrate the effectiveness of the cross disparity module in the KITTI Raw dataset and show that this method is able to detect dynamic features that are missed by the 3D object detection scheme alone.

</details>


### [219] [Cross-Platform Control for Autonomous Surface Vehicles via Adaptive Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02037)
*Ruiheng Jiang,Thomas Bi,Raffaello D'Andrea,Aswin Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应强化学习方法，通过教师-学生架构学习平台动力学的潜在表征，实现在不同自主水面飞行器上的零样本跨平台轨迹跟踪，在真实实验中比非自适应基线提升高达58%的定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主水面飞行器的水动力学和驱动特性差异大，现有控制器多为单平台设计，缺乏通用的跨平台轨迹跟踪策略。

Method: 采用部分可观测性方法，通过交互历史推断平台动力学潜在表征；使用教师-学生架构学习该表征；在随机化动力学仿真中训练策略，零样本部署到真实平台。

Result: 在两种不同真实平台上，自适应策略的定位平均绝对误差比非自适应基线降低最高58%，接近平台专用调优控制器的跟踪精度。

Conclusion: 所提方法无需高保真水动力学仿真器，仅依赖简单解析动力学模型即可实现有效的零样本跨平台部署，为多平台通用控制提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Autonomous surface vehicles vary widely in hydrodynamic and actuation characteristics, yet most controllers are designed for single-platform deployment. We present an adaptive reinforcement learning approach for trajectory tracking that enables zero-shot cross-platform deployment using a single policy. Since the deployment platform's dynamics are unknown to the policy, we address cross-platform generalization with the standard partial-observability approach of conditioning on interaction history, employing a teacher-student architecture in which a learned module infers a latent representation of the platform dynamics. The policy is trained in simulation under randomized vessel dynamics and is deployed zero-shot to two real-world platforms without any fine-tuning, despite relying on a simple analytical dynamics model rather than a high-fidelity hydrodynamic simulator. In real-world experiments on two different platforms, the adaptive policy outperforms non-adaptive learning-based baselines by up to 58% in position mean absolute error while approaching the tracking accuracy of a platform-specific tuned controller.

</details>


### [220] [Guided Action Flow: Q-Guided Inference for Flow-Matching Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02092)
*Liuhaichen Yang,Zhuang Jiang,Chenchao Sheng,Zezhi Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Guided Action Flow框架，在保持预训练SmolVLA策略冻结的情况下，通过训练动作块评论家在推理时引导反向流采样，在LIBERO操作任务上将单任务成功率从68.0%提升至82.0%，验证了Q引导推理的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配视觉-语言-动作策略通过迭代传输过程生成机器人动作块，这为无需重新训练基础策略的测试时引导提供了机会。作者研究如何利用这一机会，在不重新训练的情况下提升预训练策略的性能。

Method: 提出Guided Action Flow框架，保持预训练SmolVLA策略冻结，使用从真实成功与失败 rollout 中训练的动作块评论家，通过动作梯度在反向流采样过程中引导生成。评论家可条件化于冻结SmolVLA语言路径的任务描述特征。

Result: 在LIBERO操作任务上：单任务评论家将成功率从一个种子窗口的68.0%提升至82.0%，另一个窗口从82.0%提升至86.0%；多族任务描述评论家将验证成功率从46.0%提升至56.0%，但锁定的保留测试集提升较小（65.0%到67.5%）。

Conclusion: 结果支持对冻结的流匹配VLA策略进行Q引导推理的可行性，同时表明评论家泛化能力和不确定性感知引导仍是核心瓶颈。

Abstract: Flow-matching vision-language-action policies generate robot action chunks through an iterative transport process, creating an opportunity for test-time guidance without retraining the base policy. We study this opportunity in Guided Action Flow, an inference-time framework that keeps a pretrained SmolVLA policy frozen and uses a learned action-chunk critic to guide its reverse-time flow sampler. The critic is trained from real success and failure rollouts, can condition on task-description features from the frozen SmolVLA language pathway, and is used only through action gradients during sampling. We evaluate the approach on LIBERO manipulation tasks. A single-task critic improves success from 68.0% to 82.0% on one seed window and from 82.0% to 86.0% on another. A multi-family task-description critic improves validation success from 46.0% to 56.0%, while the locked held-out test gain is positive but modest, from 65.0% to 67.5%. These results support the feasibility of Q-guided inference for frozen flow-matching VLA policies, while showing that critic generalization and uncertainty-aware guidance remain the central bottlenecks.

</details>


### [221] [Bridge-WA: Predicting Where and How the World Changes for Robotic Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02195)
*Yongjie Bai,Hanting Wang,Mingtong Dai,Qijun Zhong,Yang Liu,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Bridge-WA提出一种轻量级世界-动作框架，通过蒸馏冻结的未来变化教师模型得到三个紧凑先验（未来标记、变化图、运动流图），在推理时移除教师模型，实现高效的与动作相关的场景变化感知，提升机器人操作任务的成功率与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型虽受益于大规模视觉-语言先验，但有效操作需要预测与动作相关的场景变化。现有世界-动作模型依赖大尺度生成模型或密集未来 rollout，计算成本高且将容量浪费在与控制耦合弱的视觉细节上。

Method: 将冻结的未来变化教师模型蒸馏为三个紧凑先验：未来标记（预期结果）、变化图（干预支持）、运动流图（局部过渡方向）。WorldBridge通过多源注意力记忆和时空偏置将这些先验条件化到动作Transformer中，推理时移除教师模型。

Result: 在VLABench、RoboTwin2.0、LIBERO-Plus及真实机器人评估中，Bridge-WA在任务成功率、进度和鲁棒性上均有提升，尤其在分布外视觉偏移下增益明显。

Conclusion: 通过聚焦动作生成于场景将如何变化，Bridge-WA抑制了背景、光照、干扰物等无关外观因素，无需部署时密集未来图像生成即可实现更好的泛化。

Abstract: General-purpose vision-language-action models benefit from large vision-language priors, but effective manipulation also requires anticipating action-relevant scene changes. Existing world-action models often rely on large generative world models or dense future rollouts, which are expensive and spend capacity on visual details weakly coupled to control. We present Bridge-WA, a lightweight world-action framework that distills a frozen future-change teacher into three compact priors: future tokens for intended outcomes, change maps for intervention support, and motion-flow maps for local transition direction. A WorldBridge conditions the action transformer on these priors through multi-source attention memories and spatial-temporal biases, while the teacher model is removed at inference. Across VLABench, RoboTwin2.0, LIBERO-Plus and real-robot evaluations, Bridge-WA improves task success, progress, and robustness, with particularly clear gains under out-of-distribution visual shifts. By focusing action generation on where and how the scene will change, Bridge-WA suppresses nuisance appearance factors such as background, lighting, and distractors, leading to better generalization without deployment-time dense future-image generation. Code and visualizations are available at: https://hcplab-sysu.github.io/BRIDGE-WA .

</details>


### [222] [Actuator Reality Shaping for Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02205)
*Satoshi Yamamori,Koji Ishihara,Kentaro Minamikawa,Kiyoharu Ohomori,Taiyo Yazaki,Norikazu Sugimoto,Jun Morimoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"执行器现实塑造"的新范式，通过对物理执行器设计双自由度前馈-反馈控制器，使其闭环行为匹配仿真中使用的理想二阶参考动力学，从而在不修改仿真器的情况下实现强化学习策略从仿真到真实机器人的零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Sim-to-real迁移中因执行器动力学非线性、硬件相关性导致仿真策略与物理机器人间存在差距的问题，避免传统方法如增加仿真器保真度、系统辨识、域随机化或学习执行器模型的高成本。

Method: 为每个关节配备双自由度前馈-反馈控制器，将参考响应整形与鲁棒镇定解耦，使物理执行器的闭环行为匹配仿真中使用的理想化二阶参考动力学，从而为强化学习策略提供标准化的执行器接口。

Result: 在单关节高传动比伺服电机（带外部负载）、7自由度机械臂到达任务中显著减少了Sim-to-real跟踪误差并提升了零样本任务性能；在轮腿机器人斜坡行驶和人形机器人行走任务中验证了零样本迁移能力。

Conclusion: 执行器现实塑造可作为跨不同硬件平台的机器人学习可复用接口，在不进行任务级微调或学习执行器模型的前提下实现零样本迁移。

Abstract: Sim-to-real transfer in robot learning is often limited by discrepancies between the ideal actuator dynamics assumed during policy training and the nonlinear, hardware-dependent behavior of physical motors. While conventional approaches attempt to bridge this gap by increasing simulator fidelity through system identification, domain randomization, or learned actuator models, we introduce an alternative paradigm: actuator reality shaping. Instead of modifying the simulator to match the real world, our method shapes the closed-loop behavior of physical actuators to match the idealized second-order reference dynamics used in simulation. By equipping each joint with a two-degree-of-freedom feedforward--feedback controller, we decouple reference-response shaping from robust stabilization, thereby providing a standardized actuator interface for reinforcement learning policies. As a result, policies trained only with the prescribed reference model can be deployed zero-shot on real hardware without task-level fine-tuning or learned actuator models. We validate the approach on a single-joint high-gear-ratio servo under external loads and a 7-DOF robotic arm reaching task, where actuator reality shaping substantially reduces sim-to-real tracking error and improves zero-shot task performance compared with standard servo-control and representative real-to-sim-to-real baselines. We further demonstrate zero-shot transfer on a wheeled-legged robot driving over a slope and a humanoid robot walking, suggesting that actuator reality shaping can serve as a reusable interface for robot learning across diverse hardware platforms.

</details>


### [223] [CoFL-S: Spatially Queryable Sector Flow Fields for Local Language-Conditioned Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02222)
*Haokun Liu,Zhaoqi Ma,Yicheng Chen,Wentao Zhang,Masaki Kitagawa,Zicen Xiong,Jinjie Li,Moju Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CoFL-S，一种低层级视觉-语言-动作框架，通过预测语言条件流场生成连续轨迹，在连续时间Habitat基准测试中优于动作token和动作chunk基线，并实现零样本真实世界部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言导航研究中高层级指令推理、记忆、全局地图构建和指令分解被广泛关注，但低层级动作表示相对未被充分探索。

Method: 提出CoFL-S框架，预测机器人局部可见区域上的语言条件流场，并通过展开预测场生成连续轨迹；将VLN-CE片段转换为帧级局部监督（含对齐的子指令、匹配的动作、轨迹和密集流场目标）；构建连续时间Habitat基准测试，隔离低层级动作接口，通过共享速度指令控制器实现闭环比较。

Result: 在匹配编码器和训练设置下，CoFL-S在连续时间Habitat基准测试中，在不同规划频率下均一致优于动作token和动作chunk基线；零样本真实世界闭环部署也展示了两者优势。

Conclusion: CoFL-S通过语言条件流场预测有效解决了低层级动作表示问题，在仿真和真实场景中均展现出优于现有基线的性能，为低层级VLN研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation has increasingly emphasized high-level instruction reasoning, memory, global map construction, and instruction decomposition, while the low-level action representation remains comparatively underexplored. We propose CoFL-S, a low-level vision-language-action framework that predicts a language-conditioned flow field over the robot's local visible sector and generates continuous trajectories by rolling out the predicted field. To train this low-level representation, we convert each VLN-CE episode, originally a whole-episode instruction paired with an action sequence, into frame-level local supervision with aligned sub-instructions and matched action, trajectory, and dense flow-field targets. For evaluation, we introduce a continuous-time Habitat benchmark that isolates low-level action interfaces from instruction decomposition and executes all methods through a shared velocity-command controller, enabling decomposition-independent closed-loop comparison across different planner frequencies rather than fixed discrete forward-and-turn transitions in VLN-CE. Under matched encoders and training settings, CoFL-S consistently outperforms action-token and action-chunk baselines across planner frequencies in the continuous-time Habitat benchmark, and zero-shot real-world closed-loop deployment further shows its advantage over both baselines beyond simulation.

</details>


### [224] [NEUROSYMLAND: Neuro-Symbolic Landing-Site Assessment for Robust and Edge-Deployable UAV Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02277)
*Weixian Qian,Tianyi Yang,Sebastian Schroder,Yao Deng,Jiaohong Yao,Xiao Cheng,Richard Han,Xi Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NEUROSYMLAND是一个神经符号着陆点评估系统，通过概率语义场景图和符号约束实现轻量级感知与显式安全推理，在72个模拟场景中取得61次成功评估，优于四个基线方法（37-57次），并在100次硬件在环试验中验证了边缘设备的可行性，符号推理仅占端到端延迟的很小部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-only学习方法在非结构化环境中易受地形变化影响且安全性决策透明度有限，需要一种可解释且鲁棒的着陆点评估方案。

Method: 构建概率语义场景图（PSSG），结合符号约束（地形平坦度、障碍物间距、空间一致性）进行结构化推理，在边缘设备上实现轻量级执行。

Result: 72个模拟场景中61次成功评估，优于基线（37-57次）；100次硬件在环试验验证了边缘部署可行性，符号推理延迟占比极小，主要开销在感知和PSSG构建。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法能提升无人机着陆点评估的鲁棒性和可解释性，且可在边缘受限硬件上部署，代码与数据集已开源。

Abstract: Safe landing-site assessment in unstructured environments remains a key challenge for autonomous UAV deployment, as vision-only learning approaches often degrade under terrain variability and provide limited transparency in safety decisions. We present NEUROSYMLAND, a neuro-symbolic landing-site assessment system that integrates lightweight perception with explicit safety reasoning. The framework constructs a probabilistic semantic scene graph from onboard visual input and evaluates candidate landing regions using symbolic constraints capturing terrain flatness, obstacle clearance, and spatial consistency, enabling structured reasoning under perceptual uncertainty while maintaining edge-feasible execution. Across 72 simulated landing scenarios spanning diverse terrains, NEUROSYMLAND achieves 61 successful assessments, outperforming four competitive baselines (37-57 successes). To evaluate deployability, we further conduct 100 hardware-in-the-loop trials with randomized initial poses, profiling end-to-end latency, stage-wise execution time, and system-level metrics including CPU/GPU utilization, memory footprint, and power consumption. Results demonstrate improved robustness and interpretability with bounded edge-resource usage. Profiling shows that symbolic reasoning contributes only a small fraction of end-to-end latency, while the main computational cost arises from perception and PSSG construction. These results demonstrate the feasibility of deploying the landing-site assessment stack on edge-constrained UAV hardware, and all source code, datasets, prompts, and symbolic rule refinement examples are released in an open-source repository

</details>


### [225] [Real-Time Visual Intelligence on Low-Cost UAVs: A Modular Approach for Tracking, Scanning, and Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02298)
*Andrei-Marian Ungureanu,Stelian Spînu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文基于DJI Tello无人机平台，集成面部检测、面部识别和单目深度估计三种AI功能，构建了一个低成本、开源的个人助理无人机系统。通过Web界面实现操控与实时监控，Python后端使用轻量级神经网络处理视觉数据。系统在人员跟踪、室内扫描和虚拟传感器自主线跟随等任务中表现良好，验证了在资源受限硬件上部署先进AI的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有商用无人机解决方案成本高、封闭性强，缺乏面向个人助理场景的轻量级、低成本、开源替代方案。作者希望验证在资源受限的嵌入式平台上部署先进AI技术的可行性，为人机协作、军事侦察、救援监视等应用提供基础。

Method: 采用模块化架构，基于DJI Tello无人机平台，集成面部检测、面部识别和单目深度估计三项核心AI功能。前端为Web界面实现无人机控制和实时视频监控，后端为Python服务器，使用轻量级神经模型进行视觉数据推理，针对嵌入式系统进行优化。

Result: 系统在真实环境下表现出稳健性能，成功实现了人员跟踪、室内扫描以及基于虚拟传感器的自主线跟随等功能。

Conclusion: 该项目验证了先进AI技术在实时机器人系统中的适用性，证明了在资源受限硬件上部署AI的可行性，为未来自主无人机在军事、救援和监视等领域的进一步研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Autonomous drones are rapidly transforming modern warfare and civil applications alike. This paper presents the development of an integrated intelligent drone system designed to serve as a personal assistant. Leveraging the DJI Tello drone platform, we implemented a modular architecture that integrates three core artificial intelligence functionalities: facial detection, facial recognition, and depth estimation from monocular vision. A web-based interface enables seamless drone control and real-time video monitoring, while a Python-based server processes visual data and executes inference pipelines using lightweight neural models optimized for embedded systems. Unlike existing commercial solutions, this system emphasizes accessibility, low-cost hardware, and open-source technologies. The system demonstrates robust performance in real-world conditions, including person tracking, indoor scanning, and autonomous line following using virtual sensors. This project validates the applicability of advanced AI techniques in real-time robotic systems and illustrates the feasibility of deploying them on constrained hardware, providing a foundation for future research in autonomous UAVs for military, rescue, and surveillance missions.

</details>


### [226] [The Moving Eye: Enhancing VLA Spatial Generalization via Hybrid Dynamic Data Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02322)
*Jincheng Tang,Yilong Zhu,Zhengyuan Xie,Jiang-Jiang Liu,Jiaxing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出通过混合连续相机运动和多样化静态视角的数据策略，解决VLA模型在空间泛化中的捷径学习问题，有效提升模型对未见相机位姿和物体配置的泛化能力，且该策略对多种架构（ACT、Diffusion、Pi0、Gr00t）均有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在机器人操作中空间泛化能力脆弱，简单地增加视角数量无法解决模型通过捷径学习利用虚假相关性（如物体间或相机与机器人基座间的固定相对位姿）而非真正空间关系的问题。

Method: 采用双机械臂设置，一只手臂执行操作，另一只作为移动环境相机；系统评估三种数据分布模式（固定视角、多固定视角、移动视角），提出混合策略：连续相机运动结合多样化静态视角，在减少虚假相关性的同时保持训练稳定性。

Result: 混合策略显著优于仅增加静态视角的方法，有效缓解了捷径学习问题，使VLA模型能够泛化到未见过的相机位姿和物体配置；且所有评估模型（ACT、Diffusion、Pi0、Gr00t）均从中受益。

Conclusion: 对捷径学习的易感性和空间泛化的困难是跨不同架构的普遍特性，而本文提出的混合数据策略提供了一种数据层面的有效解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown remarkable promise in generalized robotic manipulation. However, their spatial generalization remains fragile. We argue that simply increasing the number of viewpoints is insufficient. Models often fall into the trap of Shortcut Learning, latching onto spurious correlations (e.g., fixed relative poses between objects or between the camera and robot base) rather than learning true spatial relationships. In this work, we propose a data-centric solution to enhance VLA spatial generalization. We utilize a dual-arm setup where one arm performs manipulation while the other serves as a mobile environmental camera. We systematically evaluate three data distribution patterns: Fixed, Multi-Fixed, and Moving Views. Our findings reveal that a hybrid strategy, combining continuous camera motion with diverse static viewpoints, yields the best performance by substantially reducing spurious correlations while maintaining training stability. Our experiments demonstrate that this strategy mitigates spurious correlations, enabling VLAs to generalize to unseen camera poses and object configurations where simply adding more static viewpoints fails. Crucially, we reveal that the susceptibility to shortcut learning and the struggle with spatial generalization are universal characteristics shared across diverse architectures. Consequently, all evaluated models (ACT, Diffusion, and VLA models including Pi0 and Gr00t) benefit significantly from our mixed data strategy.

</details>


### [227] [HEFT: Heavy-Payload Full-size Humanoid Teleoperation with Privileged Motion Guidance and Windowed Payload Curriculum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02332)
*Chenxin Liu,Qingzhou Lu,Guangxiao Yang,Xuanyang Shi,Chenghan Yang,Yanjiang Guo,Jianyu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HEFT是一个面向全尺寸重型负载人形机器人的遥操作框架，通过特权运动引导（PMG）和窗口化负载课程（WPC）解决了大惯性、窄平衡裕度下VR追踪噪声和有效载荷利用不足的问题，在175cm、65kg的L7平台上实现了高达24kg负载下的运动追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动追踪与遥操作框架大多在紧凑平台上验证，未考虑真实负载交互，全尺寸人形机器人在大惯性、紧平衡裕度下对VR追踪噪声、漂移和重定向误差极为敏感，且有效载荷潜力未被充分利用。

Method: 提出HEFT框架：1) Privileged Motion Guidance (PMG)：从可部署的含噪VR参考中学习物理上合理的重构参考；2) Windowed Payload Curriculum (WPC)：结合专家引导的负载上限，逐步训练鲁棒的重载追踪能力。

Result: 在175cm、65kg的全尺寸人形机器人L7上部署，实现了包含转向、前进/后退行走、深蹲等动作的追踪，有效负载最高达24kg。

Conclusion: HEFT有效解决了全尺寸人形机器人在真实重载场景下遥操作的两个核心挑战（噪声敏感性与负载利用率不足），为大规模人形技能获取提供了可行路径。

Abstract: General motion tracking and teleoperation offer a promising path to scalable humanoid skill acquisition, yet most existing frameworks are validated on compact platforms or without real payload interaction, leaving full-size humanoids with real payloads largely unexplored. Scaling to full-size humanoids introduces two compounding challenges: their larger inertia and tighter balance margins make tracking highly sensitive to noise, drift, and retargeting errors from commodity VR trackers, while their payload potential remains largely underutilized. We present HEFT, a heavy-payload full-size humanoid teleoperation framework that addresses both challenges. HEFT learns from deployable noisy VR references with physically plausible reconstructed references through Privileged Motion Guidance (PMG), and uses a Windowed Payload Curriculum (WPC) with expert-guided payload caps to acquire robust heavy-payload tracking. We deploy HEFT on L7, a 175cm, 65kg humanoid. The robot tracks motions including turns, forward/backward locomotion, and squats under payloads up to 24kg.

</details>


### [228] [ACID: Action Consistency via Inverse Dynamics for Planning with World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02403)
*Gawon Seo,Dongwon Kim,Suha Kwak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ACID框架，通过循环动作一致性（逆动力学模型验证预测轨迹的可实现性）改进决策时规划，在多种世界模型和任务中提升规划效率与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准决策时规划仅检查预测终端状态与目标的接近程度，忽略中间状态的可实现性，导致预测轨迹看似合理但实际环境 rollout 会偏离。

Method: 提出ACID框架，引入循环动作一致性约束：利用逆动力学模型从预测的转移中反向推断动作，应与前向条件动作一致；通过尺度不变自适应权重将每步残差集成到规划成本中。

Result: 在4种动作条件世界模型和6个任务（刚体/可变形操作、关节控制、视觉导航）上，ACID一致提升规划性能，在显著减少规划计算量的情况下达到与基线相当的精度。

Conclusion: 循环动作一致性是一种有效且轻量的正则化手段，能提升决策时规划的可实现性，减少规划计算开销。

Abstract: Decision-time planning with action-conditioned world models has become a popular paradigm for embodied control. However, the standard planning cost judges a candidate solely by how close its predicted terminal state lies to the goal, leaving the realizability of the intermediate transitions unchecked -- a predicted trajectory can look convincing while the environment rollout drifts away from it. In this paper, we propose ACID, a decision-time planning framework that introduces cycle action consistency: the action inferred backward from a predicted transition by an inverse dynamics model should recover the one that was conditioned on. We fold this per-step residual into the planning cost via a scale-invariant adaptive weight. Across four action-conditioned world models and six tasks spanning rigid and deformable manipulation, articulated control, and visual navigation, ACID consistently improves planning and matches the baseline's accuracy with substantially less planning compute.

</details>


### [229] [LIME: Learning Intent-aware Camera Motion from Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02417)
*Boyang Sun,Jiajie Li,Yung-Hsu Yang,Chenyangguang Zhang,Tim Engelbracht,Sunghwan Hong,Cesar Cadena,Marc Pollefeys,Hermann Blum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LIME模型，利用第一人称视频中的多意图监督信号，将自由形式自然语言指令映射为相对SE(3)相机目标位姿，实现语言条件下的相机运动生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言导航和操作策略已能处理基座运动和机械臂操作，但语言条件下的相机运动作为一类独立动作尚未被充分探索。相机视角变化受潜在感知意图驱动，且可能在不同语义粒度上执行，需要建模这种多意图结构。

Method: 从自我中心视频中挖掘多意图相机运动监督信号，将合理的意图和观测增益描述与相对SE(3)目标位姿配对。提出LIME模型，结合自回归观测增益输出和连续流匹配位姿头，联合预测下一视角应观察到什么，同时表示多假设目标视角。

Result: 实验表明LIME能够从被动的人类视频中学习主动选择相机位姿，将普通的第一人称录像转化为有意主动感知的监督信号，并在下游机器人任务中验证了有效性。

Conclusion: LIME展示了从被动视频中学习主动、意图感知的相机运动控制的可行性，为语言条件下的主动感知提供了新范式。

Abstract: Autonomous robots often need to move their camera before they can act: to inspect an object, reveal an occluded region, or obtain a view that responds to a user's intent. While vision-language navigation translates instructions to base motion and vision-language-action policies map instructions to manipulation actions, language-conditioned camera motion remains comparatively underexplored as a first-class action. We formulate language-conditioned camera motion generation: given a current RGB observation and a free-form natural-language intent, predict a relative target camera pose for the next observation. This task is inherently non-trivial: viewpoint changes are driven by latent perceptual intentions, and a valid motion may operate at different semantic granularity, from entering a room to looking around a corner, inspecting a visible object, or revealing an occluded detail. To model this structure, we mine multi-intention camera-motion supervision from egocentric video, pairing plausible intents and observation-gain descriptions with relative SE(3) target poses. We propose LIME, a vision-language camera-motion generator that combines an auto-regressive observation-gain output with a continuous flow-matching pose head. This design lets the model jointly predict what the next view should reveal while representing multi-hypothesis target views. Across experiments and downstream robotic tasks, we show that LIME can learn to actively choose camera poses from passive human video, turning ordinary egocentric recordings into supervision for intent-aware active perception.

</details>


### [230] [WorldSample: Closed-loop Real-robot RL with World Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02431)
*Yuquan Xue,Le Xu,Zeyi Liu,Zhenyu Wu,Zhengyi Gu,Xinyang Song,Bofang Jia,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WorldSample提出了一种基于物理的增强框架，通过真实-合成循环（物理 rollout → 世界模型生成 → 策略改进）来降低真实机器人强化学习的交互成本。它引入策略节奏学习（PPL）调节训练过程，在接触丰富和精确操作任务上实现了28%的成功率提升和59%的训练步骤减少，同时世界模型的视觉保真度PSNR提升19.4dB、SSIM提升0.47。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人强化学习面临交互成本过高的问题，每次物理 rollout 都代价高昂且只能反映单一的行动-结果路径。虽然RL可以克服模仿学习在演示覆盖范围上的限制，但高昂的交互成本限制了其在真实机器人上的部署。

Method: WorldSample框架包含三个核心组件：1) 基于真实rollouts后训练的世界模型生成高保真合成转移样本；2) 策略节奏学习（PPL）通过样本选择和调度来调节训练过程，在有效增强与价值估计过高之间取得平衡，并减轻幻觉引入的噪声；3) 真实-合成闭环连接物理rollouts、世界模型生成和策略改进。

Result: 在涉及接触丰富和精确操作的机器人操作任务上，WorldSample相比基线方法将策略成功率提升28%，同时将训练步骤减少59%。世界模型的视觉保真度相比仅使用演示的后训练方法，PSNR提升19.4dB，SSIM提升0.47。

Conclusion: WorldSample通过真实-合成循环有效降低了真实机器人RL的交互成本，同时提升了策略性能和世界模型的视觉保真度，验证了该框架在真实机器人强化学习中的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) can overcome the demonstration-coverage limitation of imitation learning (IL) by allowing robots to improve through trial-and-error interaction beyond the states observed in demonstrations. However, deploying RL on real robots remains constrained by high interaction costs, since each physical rollout is costly and reflects only one realized action-outcome path. To address this challenge, we propose WorldSample, a physically grounded data augmentation framework for real-robot RL that closes a real-synthetic loop between physical rollouts, world-model generation, and policy improvement. Grounded on real rollouts, WorldSample generates high-fidelity synthetic transitions through a post-trained world model, which greatly lowers the visual hallucination. Specifically, rather than simply using these transitions as real-world experience, WorldSample introduces Policy-Paced Learning (PPL) to regulate the training process through sample selection and scheduling, balancing useful augmentation against value overestimation and mitigating the hallucination-induced noise. Experiments on robot manipulation tasks involving contact-rich and precise tasks show that WorldSample improves policy success rate by 28% while reducing training steps by 59% compared with baselines. Furthermore, WorldSample improves world model visual fidelity by 19.4dB in PSNR and 0.47 in SSIM over demonstration-only post-training, validating the effectiveness of the real-synthetic loop for both policy and world model performance.

</details>


### [231] [Learning to Move Before Learning to Do: Task-Agnostic pretraining for VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02466)
*Junhao Shi,Siyin Wang,Xiaopeng Yu,Li Ji,Jingjing Gong,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出任务无关预训练（TAP）框架，通过解耦物理能力与语义对齐两个学习目标，先利用廉价无标签交互数据（如离轨轨迹和自主机器人探索）通过自监督逆动力学目标学习可迁移的运动先验，再使用少量专家数据完成语言对齐。在SIMPLER基准上，TAP以数量级更少的标注数据匹配了百万级专家轨迹的性能，在真实WidowX平台上在相机扰动下仍保持25%成功率，而互联网规模基线降为0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型受限于专家示范数据（观测-指令-动作三元组）的稀缺性，因为大规模收集此类数据成本高昂。本文认为这一瓶颈源于混淆了两个不同的学习目标：物理能力（如何移动）和语义对齐（做什么），而只有后者需要语言监督。

Method: 提出分解假设（Decomposition Hypothesis），并基于此设计两阶段框架TAP：第一阶段通过自监督逆动力学（Inverse Dynamics）目标从廉价无标签交互数据（包括离轨任务轨迹和自主机器人探索）中学习可迁移的运动先验；第二阶段使用极少量专家数据将运动先验与语言指令对齐。

Result: 在SIMPLER基准上，TAP以数量级更少的标注数据匹配了百万级专家轨迹的性能，比标准行为克隆绝对提升10%。在真实WidowX平台上，TAP在相机扰动下保持25%成功率，而互联网规模基线降为0%。

Conclusion: 任务无关预训练能够产生鲁棒、可迁移的物理表示，为具身AI提供了一条可扩展的前进路径。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are fundamentally bottlenecked by the scarcity of expert demonstrations -- triplets of observations, instructions, and actions that are costly to collect at scale. We argue that this bottleneck stems from conflating two distinct learning objectives: acquiring physical competence (how to move) and acquiring semantic alignment (what to do). Crucially, only the latter requires language supervision. Building on this Decomposition Hypothesis, we propose Task-Agnostic Pretraining (TAP), a two-stage framework that first learns transferable motor priors from cheap, unlabeled interaction data -- including discarded off-task trajectories and autonomous robot play -- via a self-supervised Inverse Dynamics objective. A lightweight second stage then grounds these priors in language using minimal expert data. On the SIMPLER benchmark, TAP matches models trained on over 1M expert trajectories while using orders of magnitude less labeled data, yielding a 10% absolute gain over standard behavior cloning. On a real-world WidowX platform, TAP retains 25% success under camera perturbations where internet-scale baselines collapse to 0%, demonstrating that task-agnostic pretraining produces robust, transferable physical representations and offers a scalable path forward for Embodied AI.

</details>


### [232] [Learning Agile Intruder Interception using Differentiable Quadrotor Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02472)
*Michael Anoruo,Xiaoyu Tian,Abhishek Rathod,Timothy Naudet,Thomas Canchola,Eric Sturzinger,Kshitij Goel,Wennie Tabib*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅利用三维方向单位向量和拦截器状态进行拦截控制的强化学习方法，在高达10米/秒的速度下，相比使用简化质点动力学的基线方法平均提升30%性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度强化学习方法需要相对位置或距离信息，但在实际应用中如被动单目相机传感器难以直接获取这些信息，因此需要仅依赖方向信息的拦截控制策略。

Method: 利用可微分的四旋翼动力学模型，结合解析策略梯度方法，学习仅基于三维方向单位向量和拦截器状态的拦截策略。

Result: 在高达10米/秒的速度下，所提方法相比使用简化质点动力学的基线方法平均性能提升30%。

Conclusion: 仅依赖方向信息的强化学习拦截策略是可行的，且利用可微分的四旋翼动力学模型能够有效提升拦截性能。

Abstract: This paper presents a methodology for learning a control policy to intercept an intruder using the 3D direction unit vector to the intruder and the interceptor state. Prior deep reinforcement learning approaches assume either relative position or distance to the intruder is available, but this information is not readily accessible in real-world applications that employ passive, monocular camera sensors. Instead, we propose a solution that leverages an analytical policy gradient method using differentiable quadrotor dynamics to learn agile interception at speeds up to 10 m/s. The proposed approach outperforms baseline methods that utilize simplified point mass dynamics by an average of 30%.

</details>


### [233] [QuadRocket: An Aerial Robotic Testbed for Adaptive Thrust-Vector Control of Rocket-Like Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02474)
*Pedro Santos,Joel Reis,Paulo Oliveira,Carlos Silvestre*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出QuadRocket——一种基于四旋翼的火箭原型平台，通过万向节将圆柱体安装在四旋翼上形成倒立摆系统，并设计了自适应反步控制器与动态面姿态控制器，实现推力矢量控制下的轨迹跟踪与扰动补偿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为推力矢量控制（TVC）策略提供低成本、低风险的验证平台，解决实际运载火箭系统测试成本高、风险大的问题。

Method: 将耦合系统建模为单轴对称刚体，采用约化姿态表示（two-sphere）显式利用轴向对称性并解耦偏航与推力矢量方向；设计自适应反步控制器实现存在未知常值扰动下的几乎全局轨迹跟踪，通过控制点变换缓解非最小相位行为；将四旋翼视为推力矢量执行器，设计基于动态面的姿态控制器跟踪期望推力矢量，避免虚拟控制信号的显式微分。

Result: 仿真与室内运动捕捉实验均验证了精确的轨迹跟踪能力与有效的扰动补偿性能，确认QuadRocket作为推力矢量控制机器人平台的多功能性。

Conclusion: QuadRocket作为一种低成本、低风险的推力矢量控制验证平台是有效的，所提控制架构实现了良好的跟踪性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper presents QuadRocket, a quadrotor-based rocket prototype that provides a low-cost, low-risk platform for validating advanced thrust-vector control strategies for launch vehicle-type systems. The prototype consists of a cylindrical main body mounted on top of a quadrotor through a universal joint, forming a flying inverted pendulum with non-negligible inertia. For control design, the coupled system is modeled as a single axisymmetric rigid body actuated by a vectored force applied along its longitudinal axis. A reduced-attitude representation on the two sphere is adopted to explicitly exploit the vehicle's axial symmetry and to decouple yaw from the thrust-vector direction. On this model, we derive an adaptive backstepping controller that achieves almost global trajectory tracking in the presence of unknown constant disturbances, while a control-point transformation mitigates non minimum-phase behavior. The quadrotor is then treated as a thrust vector actuator, and a dynamic-surface-based attitude controller is designed to track the desired thrust-vector, accounting for actuation dynamics and avoiding explicit differentiation of virtual control signals. The complete architecture is evaluated in simulation and validated experimentally in an indoor motion-capture arena. Results demonstrate accurate trajectory tracking, effective disturbance compensation, and confirm the suitability of the QuadRocket as a versatile testbed for thrust-vector-controlled robotic vehicles.

</details>


### [234] [Controllable Sim Agents with Behavior Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02496)
*Juanwu Lu,Junyu Zhu,Ziran Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出可控神经变分智能体（CNeVA），通过闭合形式的共轭变分更新从每通道折扣回报中推断高斯行为潜变量，并利用混合通道掩码课程训练的整流流轨迹生成器实现可解释的轨迹控制。在Waymo数据集上达到竞争性真实感，同时提供速度、加速度、安全性和地图合规性等维度的单调可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实交通仿真需要既能模仿真实行为又能沿可解释轴进行控制的智能体，以实现变量隔离、边缘场景复现和自动驾驶系统安全测试。现有高排名仿真模型缺乏每通道可控性。

Method: 1) 通过闭合形式共轭变分更新从每通道折扣回报推断每智能体高斯行为潜变量；2) 采用混合通道掩码课程训练整流流轨迹生成器，支持无分类器引导；3) 提出软资格门控，用平滑指数衰减替代硬二值阈值，保留近阈值智能体的梯度信号。

Result: 在Waymo Open Motion Dataset上达到竞争性真实感；速度/加速度控制呈单调响应且无停滞诱导奖励黑客；引入软资格后安全可控性单调且显著；在上下文残差回报度量下实现可操纵地图合规性。

Conclusion: 可控指标必须与物理合理性防护措施结合解读，以避免奖励黑客混淆。CNeVA在保持真实感的同时提供了现有模型缺乏的每通道可控能力。

Abstract: Realistic traffic simulation requires agents that imitate logged behavior and can also be steered along interpretable axes. Such controllability enables engineers to isolate variables, reproduce specific edge cases, and test autonomous systems without real-world risk. We introduce Controllable Neural Variational Agents (CNeVA), a controllable simulated-agent framework that learns to infer a per-agent Gaussian behavior latent from per-channel discounted returns via a closed-form conjugate variational update, conditioning a rectified-flow trajectory generator trained on a mixed channel-mask curriculum for classifier-free guidance. To tackle scarcity in reward signals, we propose soft eligibility gates that replace hard binary thresholds with smooth exponential decay, preserving the gradient signal for near-threshold agents. On the Waymo Open Motion Dataset, CNeVA attains competitive realism on the benchmark while exposing per-channel controllability that the higher-ranked imitation models lack. Speed- and acceleration-based steering produces monotone responses without stall-induced reward hacking. Safety controllability is monotone and substantial with the introduction of soft eligibility. We manage to achieve steerable map compliance under a context-residual return measure. Furthermore, our experiment demonstrates that steering metrics must be read alongside physical-plausibility guardrails to avoid reward-hacking confounds.

</details>


### [235] [Embodied.cpp: A Portable Inference Runtime of Embodied AI Models on Heterogeneous Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02501)
*Ling Xu,Chuyu Han,Borui Li,Hao Wu,Shiqi Jiang,Ting Cao,Chuanyou Li,Sheng Zhong,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Embodied.cpp 是一个面向具身AI模型的便携式C++推理运行时，通过五层架构（输入适配器、序列构建器、骨干执行、头部插件、部署适配器）实现了多速率执行、延迟优先融合推理和可扩展算子/I/O支持，在VLA模型（HY-VLA、pi0.5）和WAM基准测试上分别实现了100.0%/91.0%的任务成功率和从312.2 MiB到88.1 MiB的显存压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身AI模型（VLA和WAM）的部署分散在各模型专属的Python栈、后端假设和机器人胶水代码中，且现有推理运行时主要为请求-响应服务设计，无法满足具身部署的运行时契约：闭环控制内的多速率执行、异构硬件上的延迟优先batch-1推理、以及超越固定token I/O的可扩展具身接口。

Method: 通过对代表性VLA模型和WAM的架构分析，提取共享执行路径并组织为五层架构：输入适配器、序列构建器、骨干执行、头部插件和部署适配器。运行时提供模块化多速率执行、延迟优先融合推理、可扩展算子与I/O支持，通过统一后端抽象实现跨异构设备、机器人和模拟器的部署。

Result: 在HY-VLA和pi0.5两个VLA模型上分别取得100.0%和91.0%的闭环任务成功率；在基于LingBot-VA Transformer块的WAM基准上，将块显存从312.2 MiB压缩至88.1 MiB。

Conclusion: Embodied.cpp在保持高精度的同时显著提升了不同具身模型架构的部署效率。

Abstract: Embodied AI models now span vision-language-action (VLA) models and world-action models (WAMs), but practical deployment remains fragmented across model-specific Python stacks, backend assumptions, and robot-side glue code, especially on heterogeneous edge devices. Existing inference runtimes are designed mainly for request-response serving and therefore do not satisfy the runtime contract of embodied deployment: multi-rate execution inside closed-loop control, latency-first batch-1 inference on heterogeneous hardware, and extensible embodied interfaces beyond fixed token I/O. We present Embodied.cpp, a portable C++ inference runtime for embodied models. Based on an architectural analysis of representative VLA models and WAMs, Embodied.cpp captures a shared execution path and organizes it into five layers: input adapters, sequence builders, backbone execution, head plugins, and deployment adapters. The runtime provides modular multi-rate execution, latency-first fused inference, and extensible operator and I/O support, enabling deployment across heterogeneous devices, robots, and simulators through one backend abstraction. We evaluate Embodied.cpp on two VLA models, HY-VLA and pi0.5, and on a preliminary WAM benchmark using a LingBot-VA Transformer block. The VLA deployments achieve successful closed-loop execution with 100.0% and 91.0% task success rates, respectively. The WAM benchmark reduces block memory from 312.2 MiB to 88.1 MiB. These results show that Embodied.cpp improves deployment efficiency while preserving high accuracy across diverse embodied model architectures.

</details>


### [236] [VT-WAM: Visual-Tactile World Action Model for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02503)
*Shuai Tian,Yupeng Zheng,Yuhang Zheng,Songen Gu,Yujie Zang,Yuxing Qin,Weize Li,Haoran Li,Wenchao Ding,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VT-WAM，一种视觉-触觉世界动作模型，通过统一的流匹配框架联合学习未来视觉预测、触觉形变预测和动作预测，在六类接触式操作任务中平均成功率达71.67%，显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触式操作需要策略对局部形变、压力、滑动和摩擦等触觉线索做出反应，但这些线索在时间上稀疏且视觉上不可见。现有视觉-触觉策略通常直接将触觉观测馈入动作预测，很少在动作生成过程中建模触觉形变动力学。

Method: VT-WAM包含两个核心创新：(1) 非对称混合变换器（MoT）注意力机制，桥接首帧视觉锚点与时序触觉动态；(2) 接触门控的动作-视觉-触觉注意力引导（AVTAG），在接触阶段鼓励动作查询依赖触觉证据。整个框架采用统一的流匹配（flow matching）范式联合训练视觉预测、触觉形变预测和动作预测。

Result: 在六类真实世界接触式操作任务中，VT-WAM平均成功率71.67%，比Fast-WAM提升26.67%，比OmniVTLA提升35.84%。消融实验表明，建模触觉形变动力学和引导接触阶段触觉注意力对接触式任务均至关重要。

Conclusion: VT-WAM通过联合建模视觉预测、触觉形变预测和动作预测，并引入非对称MoT注意力和接触门控AVTAG，有效提升了接触式操作任务的性能，验证了在动作生成过程中显式建模触觉形变动力学的重要性。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires policies to react to local deformation, pressure, slip, and friction, yet these cues are temporally sparse and often invisible in visual observations. Existing visual-tactile policies usually feed tactile observations directly into action prediction, but rarely model tactile deformation dynamics during action generation. In this paper, we introduce VT-WAM, a Visual-Tactile World Action Model that jointly learns future visual prediction, tactile deformation prediction, and action prediction within a unified flow matching framework. In particular, VT-WAM introduces (1) Asymmetric Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) attention to bridge a first-frame visual anchor with temporal tactile dynamics, and (2) contact-gated Action-Visual-Tactile Attention Guidance (AVTAG) to encourage action queries to rely on tactile evidence during contact phases. Across six real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, VT-WAM achieves a 71.67% average success rate, outperforming Fast-WAM by 26.67% and OmniVTLA by 35.84%. Ablations demonstrate that modeling tactile deformation dynamics and guiding contact-phase tactile attention are both important for contact-rich tasks. Project website: https://vt-wam.github.io/.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [237] [Multilayer Q-Matrix-Embedded Neural Network for Cognitive Diagnosis (M-QCDNet): Structure-Aware Deep Learning Architecture for Psychometric Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01278)
*Yiyao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种多层Q矩阵嵌入神经网络（M-QCDNet），通过将Q矩阵作为结构先验嵌入神经网络，结合损失函数中的L2惩罚项，实现认知诊断中可解释性与预测性能的平衡，并开发了基于对齐度的评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认知诊断模型（CDMs）具有良好的结构可解释性但缺乏神经网络的灵活性与预测能力，而深度神经网络虽预测性能强但缺乏心理测量透明性，二者难以兼顾。

Method: 将Q矩阵作为结构先验嵌入多层神经网络，通过损失函数中的L2惩罚项对不符合Q矩阵的技能激活进行惩罚；同时开发了基于对齐度的可解释性评估指标，用于量化预测技能激活与项目-技能映射的一致性。

Result: M-QCDNet在保持预测性能的同时，确保了潜在掌握状态的认知理论一致性和可解释性；提出的对齐度指标有效量化了模型的结构对齐程度。

Conclusion: M-QCDNet融合了心理测量透明性与神经网络灵活性，为课堂实践中的学习困难早期检测和掌握导向干预提供了可解释、公平且可操作的AI诊断工具。

Abstract: The research proposes a multilayer Q-matrix-embedded neural network for cognitive diagnosis (M-QCDNet), which integrates the structural interpretability of cognitive diagnostic models (CDMs) with the deep learning neural network (NN). M-QCDNet structures the item-skill relationship using the Q-matrix as a structural prior, ensuring latent mastery profiles remain interpretable and consistent with cognitive theory, followed by the proposed loss function with an L2 penalty to penalize skills not aligned with the Q-matrix and to balance predictive performance and structural alignment. Corresponding evaluation matrices, the interpretable alignment-based metrics that quantify the degree to which predicted skill activations correspond to item-level skills, were further developed. M-QCDNet offers practical benefits for classroom practice, enabling early detection of learning difficulties and supporting mastery-based interventions. By embedding diagnostic validity into model design, M-QCDNet bridges psychometric transparency and neural flexibility, advancing interpretable, fair, and actionable AI for cognitive diagnostics.

</details>


### [238] [I\textsuperscript{2}RiMA: Spectral Riemannian Representation with Temporal Attention for Mental Stress Detection based on EEG Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01279)
*Cheng He,Kunyu Peng,Shangen Han,Jinming Ma,Jinhong Ding,Likun Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为IRMANet的跨被试脑电压力检测方法，通过频域黎曼流形注意力网络解决个体差异和频率特异性问题，在三个数据集上以1.60M参数和31.95M FLOPs达到82.78%的平衡准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨被试脑电压力检测面临两个核心挑战：1）压力相关的判别模式既依赖个体又具有频率特异性；2）传统黎曼方法主要在时域建模空间协方差，忽略了神经振荡信息；3）标准时间分词方式破坏了跨片段的时序连贯性。

Method: 1）在每个频率点独立构建空间协方差矩阵并映射到SPD切空间，保留通道几何与频率特异性判别信息；2）频率聚类聚合模块选择信息丰富的频谱成分，形成与脑电节律对齐的紧凑频率簇；3）片内-片间注意力模块自适应整合局部片段级频谱动态与全局时序上下文。

Result: 在三个数据集上一致优于五个SOTA基线，平衡准确率最高达82.78%，模型参数仅1.60M，计算量31.95M FLOPs。

Conclusion: IRMANet通过频域黎曼流形建模与注意力机制，有效解决了跨被试脑电压力检测中的个体差异与频率特异问题，在准确性和效率上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Cross-subject EEG stress detection remains challenging because discriminative stress-related patterns are both subject-dependent and frequency-specific. Conventional Riemannian methods model spatial covariance mainly in the time domain, overlooking neural oscillations that are critical for high-level cognitive state decoding, while standard temporal tokenization often fragments inter-slice temporal coherence. To address these limitations, we propose \method{}, an Intra-Inter Riemannian Manifold Attention Network for EEG-based stress detection. \method{} constructs spatial covariance matrices independently at each frequency point and maps them to the SPD tangent space, preserving channel-wise geometry together with frequency-specific discriminative cues. It further introduces frequency cluster aggregation to select informative spectral components and reduce redundancy by forming compact, data-driven frequency clusters aligned with EEG rhythms. Finally, an intra-inter slice attention module adaptively integrates local slice-level spectral dynamics and global temporal context across EEG sequences. Experiments on three datasets show that \method{} consistently outperforms five state-of-the-art baselines, achieving up to 82.78\% balanced accuracy while remaining efficient with only 1.60M parameters and 31.95M FLOPs.

</details>


### [239] [Fixed-Set Robustness in Programming by Example: Example Corruption and Semantic Partition Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01280)
*Yuan Si,Jialu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了编程-by-示例（PBE）系统中的对抗性鲁棒性问题，提出了固定集最坏情况下的示例损坏形式化定义，设计了精确与启发式的损坏搜索方法，并提出了版本空间分区聚合（VPA）防御策略。实验表明，低边际PBE任务存在对抗性鲁棒性维度，而VPA仅在干净语义保持分区投票边际时有效，在现实任务中常常失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PBE系统通常假设示例错误来自随机噪声（如打字错误），但忽略了**对抗性攻击者**这一更严重的失效模式——攻击者看到合成器后有意选择最有害的示例进行破坏，使得返回的程序严重偏离预期。本文旨在揭示这一被忽视的鲁棒性维度。

Method: 1) 形式化定义了有限PBE版本空间下的**固定集最坏情况损坏**；2) 针对字符串变换DSL实现了精确（有界池内）和启发式的损坏搜索；3) 提出**版本空间分区聚合（VPA）**防御：在不相交示例组上分别合成，通过语义签名进行投票；4) 在多种数据集（人工生成任务、公开SyGuS PBE_SLIA子集、Playgol v2）上进行评估，并与随机噪声基线对比。

Result: 1) 一次精心设计的编辑即可翻转所有8个spike任务，而200次随机控制（typo、DSL池、距离匹配随机）成功率仅10.3%、11.0%、16.7%；2) 生成的高边际任务在预算1下被翻转，VPA可恢复；3) 公开SyGuS任务投票边际接近1，自适应攻击者可将VPA准确率降至零；4) Playgol上VPA在141个接受行上与随机基线有正配对bootstrap差异；5) 小规模LLM实验（20个边际-1任务）呈现定性一致的干净到攻击模式。

Conclusion: 低边际PBE任务存在对抗性鲁棒性维度，随机噪声评估无法反映这一威胁；VPA防御仅在干净语义能保持分区投票边际时有效，而现实任务中这一条件常常不满足。核心主张是有意有限且部分消极的：揭示了问题边界，但并未提供通用解决方案。

Abstract: Programming-by-example systems infer programs from a small set of input-output examples. Robust PBE work usually models wrong examples as samples from a stochastic noise process and then minimizes an expected or empirical loss. This paper studies a different failure mode: an adversary who sees the synthesizer and chooses the examples whose corruption most damages the returned program. We formalize fixed-set worst-case corruption for finite PBE version spaces, implement exact-within-bounded-pool and heuristic corruption searches for a string-transformation DSL, and introduce version-space partition aggregation (VPA), a defense that synthesizes on disjoint example groups and votes by semantic signatures. The central claim is deliberately bounded and partly negative: low-margin PBE tasks have an adversarial robustness dimension that random-typo and noisy-PBE evaluations miss, while semantic partition aggregation helps only when the clean semantics keep a partition vote margin, which often fails on realistic tasks. Evidence from curated/generated DSL tasks, accepted public SyGuS PBE_SLIA slices, SYNTRA Playgol v2, and noisy-PBE objective baselines supports that boundary. One curated edit flips all 8 spike tasks while 200-trial typo, DSL-pool, and distance-matched random controls succeed on 10.3%, 11.0%, and 16.7%; generated margin-1 rows flip under budget 1 yet VPA recovers them; on public SyGuS the vote margin is near one, so an adaptive attacker drives VPA accuracy to zero; accepted public SyGuS slices move across exact-within-pool budget boundaries; and Playgol shows positive paired-bootstrap gaps against typo and same-pool random controls on the 141 accepted rows. A small exact-output prompt harness over 20 controlled margin-1 tasks shows the same qualitative clean-to-attacked pattern across local and API models, while it is treated as a scope check, not a broad LLM benchmark.

</details>


### [240] [Domain Knowledge Based Temporal-Spatial Graph Convolution Network for ECG Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01282)
*Wenting Ma,Zhipeng Zhang,Xiaohang Yuan,Ningwei Xie,Yuxin Xie,Xiaolin Wang,Meng Guo,Xingang Chai,Zhenjie Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于领域知识的图卷积网络（Domain Knowledge-based GCN）用于心电图识别，通过双流有向图建模心电周期的空间和时间依赖关系，在首个中国心电图智能竞赛数据集上实现了88.1%的平均F1分数，对稀有类别的F1分数达76.3%，优于现有最先进模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI在医疗领域广泛应用，但模型可解释性仍然不足，尤其是在心电图识别这类专业领域。现有端到端卷积神经网络缺乏对心电图关键领域知识（如PRQST关键点）的有效利用，导致模型性能受限，尤其是对稀有类别的识别能力不足。

Method: 提出基于领域知识的图卷积网络方法：1) 将心电图关键点（PRQST）作为领域知识融入模型；2) 构建双流有向图，包括空间有向图（捕获关键点间位置关系）和时间有向图（刻画相邻心电周期的时间依赖关系）；3) 在首个中国心电图智能竞赛数据集上进行九分类任务。

Result: 在首个中国心电图智能竞赛数据集上，总体平均F1分数为88.1%，稀有类别的平均F1分数为76.3%，均优于现有最先进模型。领域知识的引入显著提升了检测性能，尤其对稀有类别效果明显。

Conclusion: 将领域知识融入图卷积网络能够有效提升心电图识别性能，特别是对稀有类别的识别能力，为AI在医疗等专业领域的可解释性和实用性提供了新的思路。

Abstract: In light of strides in Arti cial Intelligence (AI) and its wide spread application, challenges persist in the interpretability of AI models, particularly within specialized domains like healthcare, such as electro cardiograph (ECG) recognition. Rather than relying solely on end-to-end convolutional neural networks, this paper introduces a novel approach using a domain knowledge-based graph convolution network for ECG recognition. Key landmarks points of PRQST, vital to ECG interpreta tion, are incorporated as domain knowledge. The double-stream directed graph is employed to model both intra and inter ECG cycles. Speci cally, spatial directed graphs capture the positional relationships among key points, while temporal directed graphs delineate temporal dependencies between adjacent cycles in extended ECG sequences. Experimental re sults on the First Chinese ECG Intelligent Competition dataset, which speci cally classify ECG into nine categories, prove the e cacy of the proposed model. The overall average F1 score is 88.1%, the average F1 score of rare categories is 76.3%, both outperform the state-of-the-art models. The introduction of domain knowledge did enhance the detec tion performance, especially for rare categories.

</details>


### [241] [Scaling Laws for Grid-Based Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01283)
*Matthew J Liu,Wei Hang Zheng,Vidhan Purohit,Siqi Xie,Chieh-En Li,Jerry Li,Noah Flynn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了一种多探针网格算法在ANN搜索中的表现，发现在高维场景下该算法具有恒定维度缩放指数优势，且索引成本低、查询近线性扩展，在重建密集型或高维设置中具有竞争力。同时，该发现可能为高效Transformer架构的成本分析提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网格类方法在现代ANN缩放分析中缺失，现有图、树和分区方法在高维场景下吞吐量下降，需要探索网格方法的维度鲁棒性。

Method: 对多探针网格算法进行系统表征，在GloVe嵌入族上实验分析N和d的缩放行为，并与图、树、分区方法对比。

Result: 多探针网格搜索在维度缩放上保持近似恒定的指数，而其他方法吞吐量退化；查询接近线性缩放、索引成本低于竞品方法；观察到此前未报告的维度缩放交叉现象。

Conclusion: 网格方法在重建密集或高维场景下具有竞争力，其N和d缩放特性可被用于高效Transformer架构的成本分析。

Abstract: Grid-based approaches to approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) search have been absent from modern scaling analyses. We present a systematic characterization of a multiprobe grid algorithm with respect to dataset size $N$ and dimensionality $d$. Our experiments reveal a previously unreported $d$-scaling crossover on the GloVe embedding family, in which multiprobe grid search maintains an approximately constant dimensional scaling exponent while other graph-, tree-, and partitioning-based methods exhibit degrading throughput. The advantage comes with near-linear query scaling in $N$, but also with lower indexing cost than competing ANN methods. Our results suggest that grid-based methods such as multiprobe grid may be competitive in rebuild-heavy or high-dimensional settings where indexing cost and dimensional robustness dictate performance. More broadly, recent work has formalized self-attention as an ANN operation. Thus, the $N$- and $d$-scaling properties of ANN algorithms may guide cost analysis of efficient transformer architectures. Code is available at: https://github.com/weiz345/MultiProbeANN.

</details>


### [242] [IonSense-QKG: A Quantum-Readiness Metadata Framework for Lithium-Ion Battery Dataset Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01286)
*Sakthi Prabhu Gunasekar,Prasanna Kumar Rangarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出IonSense-QKG框架，通过为锂离子电池数据集添加量子就绪元数据（包括任务类型、感知模态、化学体系、量子编码候选方案、估算量子比特范围等），并引入透明量子就绪评分，将数据集选择定位为数据管理问题，为混合量子-经典机器学习工作流提供可复现的数据集发现与筛选基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公共锂电池数据集在化学体系、模态、规模、标签质量、序列结构、访问状态和预处理复杂度上差异显著，这些差异直接影响数据集是否适用于近期的混合量子-经典机器学习工作流，但缺乏一个系统性的框架来评估和发现适合量子计算研究的电池数据集。

Method: 基于EV-Battery-IonSense索引，为公共电池数据集记录丰富量子相关元数据（任务类型、感知模态、化学体系、标签可用性、序列类型、预处理需求、候选量子编码方案、估算量子比特范围及NISQ可行性），并引入透明量子就绪评分作为数据集选择的启发式方法，支持基于SQL样式的查询发现。

Result: 构建了包含元数据表、评分脚本、鲁棒性检查、链接检查工具和SQL风格查询示例的可发布工件，能够通过查询发现适用于紧凑量子特征映射、量子时间序列工作流、有限标签异常检测和未来电池健康基准测试的数据集。

Conclusion: IonSense-QKG将数据集选择定位为数据管理问题，为数据中心的量子电池分析提供了可复现的基础，但评分仅作为数据集选择启发式方法，而非量子优势的证据。

Abstract: Public lithium-ion battery datasets are increasingly used for state-of-health estimation, remaining-useful-life prediction, anomaly detection, electrochemical diagnostics, second-life analytics, and battery safety research. However, these datasets vary substantially in chemistry, modality, scale, label quality, sequence structure, access status, and preprocessing complexity. These differences directly affect whether a dataset is feasible for near-term hybrid quantum-classical machine-learning workflows.
  This paper presents IonSense-QKG, a quantum-readiness metadata framework for lithium-ion battery dataset discovery. Starting from the EV-Battery-IonSense index, the proposed framework enriches public battery dataset records with quantum-relevant metadata, including task type, sensing modality, chemistry, label availability, sequence type, preprocessing requirements, candidate quantum encodings, estimated qubit range, and NISQ feasibility. A transparent Quantum Readiness Score is introduced to rank datasets as candidate resources for future hybrid quantum-classical battery benchmarks. The score is intended as a dataset-selection heuristic, not as evidence of quantum advantage.
  The framework demonstrates query-based discovery over enriched metadata to identify datasets suitable for compact quantum feature maps, quantum time-series workflows, limited-label anomaly detection, and future battery-health benchmarking. The released artifact includes metadata tables, scoring scripts, robustness checks, link-checking utilities, and SQL-style query examples. IonSense-QKG positions dataset selection as a data-management problem and provides a reproducible foundation for data-centric quantum battery analytics.

</details>


### [243] [A Novel Machine Learning Approach for Central Nervous System Tumor Classification from DNA Methylation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01307)
*Paulo R. Ferreira,Lucas Coutinho Freitas,Laís dos Santos Gonçalves,William Borges Domingues,Lucas Petitemberte de Souza,Mariana B. Michalowski,Vinicius F. Campos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合稀疏随机投影降维与多项逻辑回归的甲基化分类方法，在CNS肿瘤分类任务中，91类级别准确率达86%，家族级别达93%，较现有最优方法分别提升约4和5个百分点，具有临床相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管DNA甲基化谱分析已成为中枢神经系统（CNS）肿瘤分类的有力手段，但在跨队列迁移性、方法学正确性以及稳健的多分类评估方面仍存在重要挑战。

Method: 提出一种方法学上更严谨的机器学习方法，将稀疏随机投影（Sparse Random Projection）用于降维，并结合多项逻辑回归（multinomial logistic regression）进行分类。

Result: 在2,801样本参考队列上，分层3折交叉验证平均准确率达96%；在1,104样本独立临床评估队列上，91类级别准确率86%，甲基化家族级别准确率93%，相较现有最优的82%和88%分别提升约4和5个百分点。

Conclusion: 所提模型在更强的机器学习方法学实践基础上，在各评估场景中一致优于此前最优方法，能够实质性提升CNS肿瘤分类的可靠性，且5个百分点的提升在临床诊断中具有实际意义，可直接影响癌症亚型分配及后续治疗决策。

Abstract: NA methylation profiling has become a powerful approach for central nervous system (CNS) tumor classification, yet important challenges remain regarding cross-cohort transferability, methodological correctness, and robust multiclass evaluation. In this work, we propose a novel and methodologically rigorous machine-learning approach for methylation-based CNS tumor classification that combines Sparse Random Projection for dimensionality reduction with multinomial logistic regression for classification. We evaluate the proposed approach in the same general experimental setting established by a widely used reference classifier. On the 2,801-sample reference cohort, our method achieves a mean accuracy of 96\% under stratified 3-fold cross-validation. On the independent 1,104-sample clinical evaluation cohort, it reaches 86\% accuracy at the 91-class level and 93\% when predictions are evaluated at the methylation class family level. These results improve upon the corresponding state-of-the-art reference figures of 82\% class-level concordance and 88\% family-level concordance, yielding absolute gains of approximately 4 and 5 percentage points, respectively. This improvement is clinically relevant: in a diagnostic setting, a 5-point increase in correct tumor classification can directly affect cancer subtype assignment and, in turn, influence treatment selection and downstream clinical decision-making. Our results show that the proposed model, grounded in stronger methodological practice in machine learning, consistently outperforms the previous state of the art across evaluation settings and can materially improve the reliability of CNS tumor classification.

</details>


### [244] [From Approximation to Emergence: A Theory of Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01311)
*Zhilin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本书《从近似到涌现》构建了一个统一、基于证明的现代深度学习理论体系，从经典的逼近、优化和泛化理论出发，逐步延伸到过参数化、鲁棒性、生成建模、Transformer、上下文学习、缩放定律、可解释性、对齐和涌现等当代机制，为深度学习理论提供了严谨的学术地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习已经超越了任何单一数学解释的范畴，现有文献分散且缺乏统一的理论框架，作者旨在将广泛的研究成果组织成连贯的研究叙事，系统梳理各理论的控制对象、有效假设及未解释现象。

Method: 采用基于证明的论述方式，按照"控制对象-有效假设-未解释现象"的三元框架审视每个理论，从经典基础（逼近、优化、泛化）逐步过渡到当代机制（过参数化、鲁棒性、生成建模、Transformer、上下文学习、缩放定律、可解释性、对齐、涌现）。

Result: 形成了一部面向研究者、研究生和受过数学训练的从业者的专著，提供了深度学习理论当前的严谨地图，展现出该理论体系"强大但不完整"的特征，并日益聚焦于学习机制如何从规模、数据、架构和训练中涌现这一核心问题。

Conclusion: 深度学习理论是一个强大但不完整的领域，其核心研究方向正逐步汇聚到"涌现"这一关键问题上，即学习机制如何从规模、数据、架构和训练的相互作用中自然产生。

Abstract: Deep learning has outgrown any single mathematical explanation. From Approximation to Emergence develops a unified, proof-oriented account of modern deep learning theory, tracing a path from the classical foundations of approximation, optimization, and generalization to the contemporary mechanisms of overparameterization, robustness, generative modeling, transformers, in-context learning, scaling laws, interpretability, alignment, and emergence. Rather than presenting isolated results, the book organizes a broad literature into a coherent research narrative: each theory is examined through the object it controls, the assumptions that make it valid, and the phenomena it leaves unexplained. Written for researchers, graduate students, and mathematically trained practitioners, this monograph offers a rigorous map of deep learning theory as it stands today: powerful, incomplete, and increasingly centered on the question of how learned mechanisms arise from scale, data, architecture, and training.

</details>


### [245] [Black-Box Inference of LLM Architectural Properties with Restrictive API Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01313)
*Christopher Ellis,Shreyas Chaudhari,Mei-Yu Wang,Leighton Barnes,Giulia Fanti,José M. F. Moura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 NightVision 攻击方法，能在当前严格限制的 LLM API（仅暴露单个 logit）下，通过共集提示技术和首令牌时间测量，以 23% 以内误差估计隐藏维度，53% 以内误差估计深度和参数量，证明现有 API 无法完全隐藏模型架构信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业 LLM 提供商在发现 API 可能泄露架构信息后，进一步限制了 API，仅暴露单个 logit 且取消 logit bias 功能。作者希望证明即使在这种严格限制下，攻击者仍能恢复关键的模型架构参数（隐藏维度、深度、参数量），揭示当前 API 的安全不足。

Method: NightVision 包含两个核心组件：(1) **共集提示（common set prompting）**：设计多个提示，使模型对同一组输出 token 返回 log 概率，对结果进行谱分析以推断隐藏维度；(2) **端到端首令牌时间（TTFT）测量**：结合估计的隐藏维度，通过测量生成第一个 token 的时间来估计模型深度和总参数量。

Result: 在 32 个开源 LLM 上评估：隐藏维度估计平均相对误差 23%（MoE 模型为 9%）；对于超过 30 亿参数的模型，深度和参数量估计误差在 53% 以内。通过消融实验展示了精度随 token 预算和模型属性的变化规律。

Conclusion: 当前 LLM API 的限制不足以完全隐藏底层模型的架构细节，提供商需要进一步加强 API 安全措施以防止架构信息泄露。

Abstract: In practice, most commercial LLM providers do not publicly release details of underlying LLM architectures. However, prior work has shown that given limited API access to an LLM (namely, top-$k$ logits and/or a logit bias function), one can recover certain architectural details of an LLM, such as the hidden dimension of the feed-forward network. Perhaps in response to these results, most commercial LLM providers have restricted their APIs to expose only the single logit for each decoded token, and they no longer give users the ability to bias logits. We show that even under current restrictive APIs, several architectural parameters are still recoverable. We present NightVision, an attack that uses restrictive black-box API access to estimate the hidden dimension, depth, and parameter count of an LLM. Algorithmically, NightVision relies on a novel common set prompting technique in which multiple prompts expose log probabilities for the same set of output tokens; a spectral analysis of these results is used to infer hidden dimension. NightVision additionally uses end-to-end time to first token (TTFT) measurements and the estimated hidden dimension to estimate depth and parameter count. We empirically evaluate NightVision on 32 open-source LLMs, recovering hidden dimension to within 23% average relative error across all models (9% on MoE models), and depth and parameter count to within 53% for models exceeding three billion parameters. We run extensive ablations to demonstrate how these accuracies scale with token budget and model properties. Overall, our results suggest that current LLM APIs are not sufficiently restricted to fully obfuscate the architectural details of their underlying models.

</details>


### [246] [Multi-modal Rail Crossing Safety Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01365)
*Paimon Goulart,Chansong Lim,Nícolas Roque dos Santos,Yue Dong,Sheldon Peterson,Jia Chen,Evangelos E. Papalexakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多模态AI管道，利用铁路道口的视觉图像和结构化事故报告数据（如FRA官方记录），进行安全评估和风险评分。该系统通过路由微调的紧凑型VLM管道，在识别高风险/低风险道口时达到0.757的宏F1分数，FRA安全评分预测的RMSE为0.078、相关系数为0.492，且定性结果与专家评估一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否仅凭铁路道口图像进行安全评估，以及引入结构化数据（如官方事故报告）能否提升评估能力，最终构建一个能够融合多模态数据、提供与专家意见及FRA安全评分一致的安全评估AI系统。

Method: 提出一个端到端的证明性管道，涵盖数据准备、多模态学习范式（视觉图像+结构化事故数据），采用路由微调的紧凑型视觉-语言模型（VLM）进行处理。

Result: 高风险/低风险道口识别宏F1=0.757；FRA安全评分预测RMSE=0.078，相关系数=0.492；定性结果与领域专家评估一致。

Conclusion: 所提出的多模态管道能够有效利用视觉和结构化数据实现铁路道口安全评估，与专家判断和官方标准保持较好一致性，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Given one or more images of a railway crossing, can we leverage visual cues that allow us to robustly estimate how safe it is? Can we improve our ability to do so by introducing structured data (such as official accident reports) about the accident history of that crossing into our models? In this work, we explore how to best answer those questions towards building an AI system that can ingest multi-modal data for railway crossings and provide safety assessment and scores that align with expert opinion and with safety scoring used by the Federal Railroad Administration (FRA). To that end, we propose a proof-of-concept pipeline that delivers on that goal, while at the same time exploring and tackling a number of critical research challenges that pertain to different parts of the pipeline, from data preparation to different learning paradigms that can allow us to realize such a system. Indicatively, our proposed system identifies HIGH-RISK and LOW-RISK crossings with a macro F1 score of 0.757 and estimates FRA-based safety scores with an RMSE of 0.078 and correlation of 0.492 using a routed fine-tuned compact VLM pipeline, while producing qualitative results that align with domain-expert assessment.

</details>


### [247] [How Should Transformers Encode Numeric Values in Electronic Health Records?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01391)
*Maria Elkjær Montgomery,Christian Igel,Mikkel Odgaard,Martin Sillesen,Mads Nielsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了Transformer模型中离散、连续和混合三种数值编码策略在电子健康记录数据上的表现，发现混合令牌方法（结合分箱与投影）在精度与鲁棒性之间取得最佳平衡，且最优分箱数遵循数据集大小的幂律分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于Transformer的序列处理中，特别是在电子健康记录数据中，如何有效编码数值是一个未被充分研究的基础性问题。现有方法缺乏系统性比较，无法明确不同编码策略在精度、稳定性和灵活性之间的权衡。

Method: 在真实EHR数据中嵌入合成算术任务，并结合真实临床预测任务，系统比较离散、连续和混合三种数值编码策略。混合方法在保留数值的同时，在投影前应用分箱处理。

Result: 显式建模值-概念交互的方法在精度敏感任务上表现最佳但受架构约束；混合令牌方法（分箱+投影）提供更稳健的替代方案，最优分箱数遵循数据集大小的幂律分布；模型表现出"足够好"的数值计算而非精确算术。

Conclusion: 在实践中，鲁棒性和可部署性通常优于最大数值精度，建议将混合令牌方法作为实用默认方案。临床任务中纳入实验室值的收益具有任务依赖性。

Abstract: How do we encode numeric values in transformer-based sequence processing, particularly in electronic health record (EHR) data? We systematically compare discrete, continuous, and hybrid value encoding strategies using synthetic arithmetic tasks embedded within real-world EHR data, as well as real-world clinical prediction tasks. Our study reveals trade-offs between numeric precision, optimisation stability, and architectural flexibility. We find that approaches that explicitly model value-concept interactions perform best on precision-sensitive arithmetic tasks when architectural constraints permit. Hybrid token-based approaches that retain numeric values but apply binning prior to projection provide a more robust and broadly applicable alternative, with the optimal number of bins following a simple empirically derived power-law in dataset size. Across tasks, models consistently exhibit reliable "good enough" numeric computation rather than exact arithmetic, while clinical gains from incorporating laboratory values are task-dependent. This suggests that robustness and deployability often outweigh maximal numeric precision in practice, motivating hybrid token-based approaches as a practical default.

</details>


### [248] [NeuroBridge: Bridging Multi-Task MRI Knowledge for Neurodegenerative Disease Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01401)
*Mengyu Li,Guoyao Shen,Chad W. Farris,Xin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuroBridge是一个基于临床引导的多任务MRI框架，通过整合大规模自监督预训练、海马分割、萎缩分类和重建任务，显著提升了阿尔茨海默病及相关痴呆症的MRI诊断性能（ADNI上88.17%准确率），并展现出强大的跨队列泛化能力和概率驱动型机会性筛查可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于MRI的阿尔茨海默病、轻度认知障碍及相关痴呆症诊断面临挑战，因为疾病相关的结构变化往往细微且具有异质性，现有单任务方法难以充分捕捉这些复杂特征。

Method: NeuroBridge框架包括：1）大规模自监督MRI预训练；2）多任务学习，联合优化海马分割、海马萎缩分类和图像重建目标；3）门控融合微调策略，整合多任务表征。

Result: 在ADNI和OASIS队列上，AD vs 认知正常对照分类准确率分别达88.17%和82.78%。在MCI相关及混合诊断场景中性能提升最为显著。框架展现出良好的跨队列泛化能力，预测概率与准确性之间存在系统性关联，验证了概率驱动型机会性筛查的可行性。

Conclusion: 临床引导的多任务表征学习优于传统单任务方法，NeuroBridge为痴呆症评估和MRI机会性筛查提供了一个鲁棒且可扩展的框架。

Abstract: INTRODUCTION: Accurate MRI-based identification of Alzheimer's disease (AD), mild cognitive impairment (MCI), and related dementias remains challenging because disease-related structural changes are often subtle and heterogeneous. We developed NeuroBridge, a clinically guided multi-task MRI framework for neurodegenerative disease diagnosis. METHODS: NeuroBridge integrates large-scale self-supervised MRI pretraining with hippocampal segmentation, hippocampal atrophy classification, and reconstruction objectives, followed by gated fusion fine-tuning. Performance was evaluated across ADNI and OASIS cohorts, including cross-cohort transfer, probability-based analysis, and opportunistic screening. RESULTS: NeuroBridge achieved the highest performance across evaluated classification tasks, reaching 88.17% accuracy for AD versus cognitively normal controls in ADNI and 82.78% in OASIS. The largest gains occurred in MCI-related and mixed-diagnosis settings. The framework demonstrated strong cross-cohort generalization, systematic associations between predicted-class probability and accuracy, and the feasibility of probability-based opportunistic screening. DISCUSSION: Clinically guided multi-task representation learning improves neurodegenerative MRI diagnosis beyond conventional single-task approaches. NeuroBridge provides a robust and scalable framework for dementia assessment and MRI-based opportunistic screening.

</details>


### [249] [Spin-Weighted Spherical Harmonics Enable Complete and Scalable $\mathrm{E}(3)$-Equivariant Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01408)
*Chenxing Liang,Yuchao Lin,Andrii Kryvenko,Wendi Yu,Chuan Li,Jianwen Xie,Xiaofeng Qian,Shuiwang Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SpinGTP通过将Gaunt张量积从标量函数推广到自旋加权球谐函数，恢复了被GTP丢失的反对称交互路径，在保持GTP渐近效率的同时实现了与完整CGTP相当的精度，特别在涉及手性材料和非中心对称几何的任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的E(3)等变网络受限于Clebsch-Gordan张量积(CGTP)的O(L^6)复杂度，虽然Gaunt张量积(GTP)降低了复杂度，但无法捕捉反对称路径，导致表达不完整。

Method: 将GTP从标量函数推广到自旋加权球谐函数(SWSH)，利用SWSH的代数性质恢复缺失的反对称相互作用，同时保持GTP的渐近效率；构建能自然处理张量积中奇宇称分量的更具表达力的等变基。

Result: 在Tetris、3BPA、SPICE-MACE-OFF和OC20等多个基准测试中，SpinGTP达到了与完整CGTP相当的精度；在涉及手性材料和非中心对称几何的任务中表现更优。

Conclusion: SpinGTP提供了一条完整、可扩展且数学严谨的路径，用于大规模3D原子系统模拟中的高阶等变性。

Abstract: $\mathrm{E}(3)$-equivariant networks are promising for 3D atomistic system modeling, yet their scalability is limited by the $O(L^6)$ complexity of the Clebsch-Gordan Tensor Product (CGTP). The recently proposed Gaunt Tensor Product (GTP) reduces the complexity but is unable to capture the antisymmetric paths, resulting in incomplete expressivity. In this work, we present SpinGTP, an approach to overcome the GTP incompleteness by generalizing from scalar functions to Spin-Weighted Spherical Harmonics (SWSH). By relying on the algebraic properties of SWSH, SpinGTP recovers the missing antisymmetric interactions while maintaining the asymptotic efficiency of GTP. It also allows for a more expressive equivariant basis that naturally accounts for the parity-odd components of tensor products. We evaluate SpinGTP across diverse benchmarks, including Tetris, 3BPA, SPICE-MACE-OFF, and OC20. Our results show that SpinGTP achieves accuracies comparable to full CGTP. Notably, by explicitly capturing antisymmetric paths, SpinGTP exhibits superior performance in tasks involving chiral materials and non-centrosymmetric geometries. This work provides a complete, scalable, and mathematically rigorous path toward high-order equivariance in large-scale 3D atomistic system simulations.

</details>


### [250] [The Rollout Infrastructure Tax in Coding-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01415)
*Daniel Thi Graviet,Lovre Pesut,Ivan Dagelic,Vedran Jukic,Ivan Burazin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了四种代码智能体强化学习执行环境（单容器、托管沙箱、Kubernetes容器、云虚拟机）的性能差异，发现冷启动延迟存在高达110倍的变化，在100万条150步轨迹的场景下，预估工时差异达1.8倍。作者主张将执行环境优化纳入训练系统设计而非仅视为部署管道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体强化学习将执行基础设施视为背景实现细节，忽略了大规模交互式软件部署中基础设施开销对训练效率的累积影响，作者意图揭示并量化这种被忽视的效率优化机会。

Method: 对四种执行环境（单容器、托管沙箱、Kubernetes编排容器、云虚拟机）进行对比实验，测量冷启动延迟和在大规模RL后训练场景（一百万条150步轨迹）下的预估工时。

Result: 四种执行环境间冷启动延迟差异高达110倍，在百万条150步轨迹场景下预估工时差异达1.8倍。

Conclusion: 未来的编码智能体RL系统应将执行环境优化作为训练系统本身的一部分来设计，而非仅仅视为部署的辅助管道。

Abstract: Coding-agent reinforcement learning treats execution infrastructure as a background implementation detail, despite relying on large numbers of interactive software rollouts. This is a missed opportunity: measuring infrastructure overhead can reveal practical efficiency gains for RL post-training, where small per-rollout savings compound at scale. We present a comparative study of four execution substrates: single containers, hosted sandboxes, Kubernetes-orchestrated containers, and cloud virtual machines. We find up to $110\times$ variation in cold-start latency and a $1.8\times$ spread in projected worker-hours for one million 150-step trajectories. Our results suggest that future coding-agent RL systems should optimize execution substrates as part of the training system itself, not merely as deployment plumbing.

</details>


### [251] [Conditional Inference Trees and Forests for Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01417)
*Robert Milletich,Justin Downes,Steve Goley,Newel Hirst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了条件推理树（CIT）和条件推理森林（CIF）作为top-k特征排序方法的性能，通过真实数据基准、运行时消融实验和合成特征恢复实验，分析了其计算开销与预测性能的权衡。CIF在17种分类方法中排名第4，18种回归方法中排名第3；消融实验表明自适应停止和阈值搜索数量对运行时间影响最大，而下游评分变化不超过0.011。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件推理树/森林通过测试特征后再选择分裂阈值来减少分裂选择偏差，但重复的置换检验和阈值搜索导致计算开销很大。本文旨在系统评估CIT/CIF作为top-k特征排序方法在预测任务中的实际表现与计算效率。

Method: 使用真实数据基准、运行时消融实验和合成特征恢复实验对CIT/CIF进行评估。分析了在固定节点下，若特征和置换预算与节点响应无关，Bonferroni校正的+1蒙特卡洛置换p值可控制节点级错误拒绝率。通过消融实验考察自适应停止、阈值搜索数量等因素对运行时的影响。

Result: CIF在17种分类方法的22个数据集上排名第4，在18种回归方法的8个数据集上排名第3。关闭自适应停止和使用精确阈值搜索分别使拟合时间增加4.0-8.4倍和1.9-10.8倍，但下游评分变化最多不超过0.011。高维稀疏模拟表明森林特征采样可能使有信息量的特征无法参与许多分裂决策。

Conclusion: 整体结果支持CIF作为top-k特征排序方法在评估的下游预测基准中具有良好的性能表现。

Abstract: Conditional inference trees (CIT) and conditional inference forests (CIF) reduce split-selection bias by testing features before choosing split thresholds, but repeated permutation tests and threshold searches can make these methods computationally expensive. We study CIT and CIF as top-$k$ feature-ranking methods for downstream prediction using real-data benchmarks, runtime ablations, and synthetic feature-recovery experiments. At a fixed node, if the features and permutation budget do not depend on the node responses, Bonferroni-corrected $+1$ Monte Carlo permutation $p$-values control nodewise rejection under the complete permutation null. CIF ranks 4th among 17 classification methods on 22 datasets and 3rd among 18 regression methods on 8 datasets. With Bonferroni correction held fixed, the CIF runtime ablations indicate that adaptive stopping and the number of thresholds searched have the largest measured effect on runtime: turning off adaptive stopping and using exact threshold search increase fitting time by 4.0--8.4$\times$ and 1.9--10.8$\times$, respectively, while downstream score changes are at most 0.011. Sparse high-$p$ simulations indicate that forest feature sampling can leave informative features out of many split decisions. Overall, the results support CIF as a top-$k$ feature-ranking method in the evaluated downstream prediction benchmarks.

</details>


### [252] [On the Utility and Factual Reliability of Pruned Mixture-of-Experts Models in the Biomedical Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01444)
*Atsuki Yamaguchi,Szymon Palucha,Léo Bijar,Aline Villavicencio,Nikolaos Aletras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了MoE模型在领域特定的专家剪枝对实用性和可靠性的影响，发现适度剪枝可保持领域内效用且不立即降低可靠性，但极端剪枝会增加幻觉风险，跨域时效用和可靠性均快速下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型虽能加速推理但内存需求大，结构化专家剪枝可降低部署成本，但现有研究主要评估基准效用，忽视了剪枝对事实可靠性的影响，尤其是在生物医学等高风险领域。

Method: 评估了四种MoE模型、六种剪枝方法、多种剪枝比率，在领域内（生物医学）和跨域设置下进行生成和分类任务测试。

Result: 适度剪枝可保持领域内效用且不立即降低可靠性，但极端剪枝比率下幻觉风险增加；跨域时效用和可靠性均快速下降。

Conclusion: 安全压缩高度依赖于任务和领域，仅凭效用评估剪枝后的MoE模型不足以判断其在高风险部署中的安全性，必须同时评估可靠性。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models offer inference speedups via selective activation but impose substantial memory requirements because the whole network must remain loaded. Structured expert pruning is a practical approach for reducing deployment costs in resource-constrained settings. However, prior studies primarily evaluate benchmark utility, leaving the effect of pruning on factual reliability underexplored, particularly in high-stakes domains such as biomedicine. In this paper, we investigate how domain-specific expert pruning affects both utility and reliability. We assess four MoE models, six pruning methods, and multiple pruning ratios across generation and classification tasks under in-domain (biomedical) and cross-domain settings. Results reveal that moderate pruning preserves in-domain utility without immediate reliability decline, although hallucination risks increase at extreme pruning ratios. When shifting to the general domain, both utility and reliability degrade rapidly. These findings indicate that safe compression depends heavily on the task and domain. Evaluating pruned MoE models solely on utility is inadequate for high-stakes deployment without reliability assessment.

</details>


### [253] [Geometry-Aware R-Structured Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01449)
*Sergei Kucherenko,Nilay Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种几何感知的R-结构KAN网络（GRS-KAN），通过将Rvachev的R-函数集成到KAN框架中，实现了平滑非线性学习与几何/逻辑约束的显式编码相结合，在含间断回归问题上相比标准KAN最多降低67%测试RMSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准KAN缺乏显式编码几何约束和逻辑结构的能力，难以处理具有间断、可行区域和隐式几何边界的回归问题，亟需一种能够融合先验几何知识的可训练神经架构。

Method: 将Rvachev的R-函数（R-合取与R-析取）作为可微逻辑操作嵌入KAN框架，提出加法型、乘法型和不可知分支加权型三种GRS-KAN变体，使KAN分支学习平滑非线性结构，R-函数分支编码已知几何/逻辑约束。

Result: 在含圆形和矩形支撑的间断回归基准上，GRS-KAN相比标准KAN降低测试RMSE最高达67%，边界定位精度显著提升；不可知变体可自动判断几何先验是否对当前学习任务有益。

Conclusion: 显式几何编码能同时提升预测精度和模型可解释性，GRS-KAN为融合符号几何知识与数据驱动学习的混合建模提供了有效范式。

Abstract: We propose a novel hybrid neural architecture, the Geometry-aware R-Structured Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (GRS-KAN), which integrates V.L.Rvachev's R-functions into the Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (KAN) framework. The proposed approach combines two complementary modeling mechanisms: smooth nonlinear structure is learned by KAN branches, while known geometric or logical constraints are encoded analytically using differentiable R-functions. This enables explicit representation of discontinuities, feasible regions, and implicit geometric boundaries within a trainable neural architecture.
  The framework implements differentiable logical operations through R-conjunctions and R-disjunctions, allowing complex geometric supports to be represented analytically and incorporated directly into regression models. Several GRS-KAN variants are introduced, including additive, multiplicative, and agnostic branch-weighted architectures.
  The method is demonstrated on regression problems involving discontinuities with circular and rectangular supports. Numerical experiments show that explicit geometric encoding substantially improves predictive accuracy and boundary localization compared with standard KANs. In the considered benchmarks, geometry-aware GRS-KAN models reduce test RMSE by up to 67% while simultaneously improving interpretability through explicit analytical representation of the learned geometric structure. The agnostic variant further demonstrates the ability to automatically determine whether geometric priors are beneficial for a given learning task.

</details>


### [254] [Token Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01455)
*Kathan Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Ember优化器，专门针对语言模型的embedding表和LM-head矩阵进行优化，利用其独特的梯度几何特性，以极低的显存开销（O(V+D)而非Adam的O(2VD)）在监督微调、强化学习和预训练中改进Pareto前沿，并发现token优化轨迹可由简单1D射线描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型中embedding表和LM-head作为连续程序与离散符号之间的读写接口，其梯度几何特性与密集隐藏权重截然不同，但现有优化器（如Adam）未针对这一特性进行优化，导致显存浪费且无法充分利用该接口的优化潜力。

Method: 提出Ember轻量级优化器，专门针对embedding和LM-head矩阵，显存复杂度为O(V+D)而非Adam的O(2VD)；无需对token表优化器状态进行分片；可无缝集成到ZeRO/FSDP分布式训练框架中。

Result: Ember在监督微调、强化学习和预训练中均能改进Pareto前沿；跨批大小和参数量有效扩展；发现token优化轨迹可由简单1D射线描述，挑战了神经网络参数位于严重非凸景观中的普遍认知。

Conclusion: embedding和LM-head的优化具有独特的几何结构，Ember利用这一特性以更低资源消耗实现更优性能；同时对Transformer训练所需优化器的空间提供了新的原则性理解。

Abstract: Language models learn continuous programs over discrete symbols, with the embedding table and LM-head acting as the read/write interface between them. We show that this interface has gradient geometry distinct from dense hidden weights which can be exploited to improve the Pareto frontier across supervised finetuning, RL, and pretraining, while only utilizing kilobytes of optimizer state. We introduce Ember, a lightweight optimizer for embedding and LM-head matrices that utilizes O(V + D) VRAM, instead of Adam's O(2VD), and forgoes the need to shard both token table optimizer states. We provide empirical evidence that Ember scales effectively across batch size and parameter count. We show that the optimization trajectory of tokens can be well described by a simple 1D ray, counter to the popular belief that neural net parameters navigate a heavily nonconvex landscape. We provide a principled view on the surprisingly narrow space of optimizers that suffice for Transformer training. Finally, we open-source our distributed Ember implementation that merges cleanly with existing ZeRO/FSDP setups to support further research at https://github.com/katop1234/ember

</details>


### [255] [Class-Grouped Normalized Momentum and Faster Hyperparameter Exploration to Tackle Class Imbalance in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01474)
*Haemin Park,Diego Klabjan,Martin W. Braun,Xiuqi Li,Balakrishnan Ananthanarayanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedCGNM（联邦类分组归一化动量）和FedHOO（基于X-armed bandit的超参优化算法），分别解决联邦学习中类别不平衡的优化问题和小规模客户端下的重采样率调优问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准集中式技术因隐私和异构性约束无法直接应用于联邦学习，而类别不平衡导致少数类预测性能差。

Method: FedCGNM：基于最小组内方差将类别分组，每组维护动量并归一化为单位长度，求和作为更新方向；FedHOO：基于X-armed bandit，利用联邦并行性以线性成本评估每个客户端的两种候选重采样率组合。

Result: 在四个公开长尾基准和一个专有芯片缺陷数据集上，FedCGNM持续优于基线，FedHOO在小规模联邦中带来进一步增益。

Conclusion: FedCGNM通过类分组归一化动量平衡多数类与少数类梯度量级并缓解稀有类梯度噪声，FedHOO高效优化重采样率，两者共同提升了FL在不平衡数据下的性能。

Abstract: Class imbalance poses a critical challenge in federated learning (FL), where underrepresented classes suffer from poor predictive performance yet cannot be addressed by standard centralized techniques due to privacy and heterogeneity constraints. We propose FedCGNM (Federated Class-Grouped Normalized Momentum), a client-side optimizer in FL that partitions classes into a small number of groups based on minimum within-group variance, maintains a momentum per group, normalizes each group momentum to unit length, and uses the summation of the normalized group momentums as an update direction.
